# Board header notes archived 2026-11-13 (owner directives and Lead notes 18 Oct - 13 Nov; superseded by DELIVERY_MODE.md where they differ)

# Task board (Lead writes; workers claim one row; verifier records the verdict)

Drive limit (lead 2026-10-18): the Google Drive connector downloads files up to 10 MB only; larger private files must be on Drive as .zip.
Parallel team (owner, 2026-10-18): 3 workers hourly, verifier hourly, lead every 2 h; 8-12 TODO, no two CLAIMED tasks on one engine file (TEAM.md "Parallel work").
Plan, milestones and daily targets: PLAN.md (owner, 2026-10-18). The engine is the product; Alsenan is one sample.
Status: QUEUED -> TODO -> CLAIMED (worker, branch) -> READY (worker done) -> MERGED or REJECTED (verifier, reason).
BLOCKED-DATA = needs `urban-boq-private-data`; BLOCKED-OWNER = needs an answer in OWNER_QUESTIONS.md.
Order: lowest gate first, then money at risk.
OWNER RULE 2026-11-13 14:30 (main chat; PLASTER HEIGHT; supersedes R-T556-1 H = interval - b 0.05 - slab 0.16). Owner: "4.5 and 4.2 is from slab to slab as we measured correct? which means depends on slab thickness for example 16cm. minus 4.2 so we get 4.04 plastering correct, check it." CHECK by the main chat: the printed levels are finished-floor levels (+1.00 / +5.50 / +9.70 / +13.90), storeys 4.50 / 4.20 / 4.20; the engine today uses H = storey - 0.05 floor build-up - 0.16 slab = 4.29 (GF) / 3.99 (1F, 2F), i.e. plaster from the FINISHED floor. The owner's rule starts plaster at the STRUCTURAL slab top (behind the floor build-up): H = storey - slab thickness = 4.50 - 0.16 = 4.34 (GF), 4.20 - 0.16 = 4.04 (1F / 2F), slab thickness as drawn for each panel. LEAD: new rule version of the plaster height (UP-FIN-PLASTER-HEIGHT, owner), regression test (normal 4.20 storey, 0.16 slab -> 4.04; adverse: a different drawn slab thickness changes H), wire into the candidate with the next plaster / paint round, report before / after in m2 (expected about +5 cm x every plastered wall run, roughly +1 %); paint and wall-tile heights follow their own rules (paint to the false ceiling, tiles as drawn).
OWNER CLARIFICATION 2026-11-13 14:00 (main chat; refines 12:00 item (1) and 13:30 items (1)(e) and (2)). Owner: "5, not maxed 1m^2 for ignored i mean if there was an ignored (calculate by calculator the area manually) and for small peices if you know its under 0.05m^3 ignore them and add a safety of 1m^3 if they do not even reach 1m^3." "3. company default is correct however alsenan is 4m and 4.5m which will increase concrete, walls blocks, plastering, paint, ceramic on bathroom and walls etc." (1) SMALL PIECES: only a piece KNOWN to be below 0.05 m3 is ignored (logged by line id); anything larger that geometry cannot read is CALCULATED BY HAND (dimensions x calculator, manual PDF read), never ignored. SAFETY: if the ignored pieces together stay below 1 m3, the BOQ adds one 1 m3 safety line; if they reach 1 m3 or more, it adds their sum. (2) HEIGHTS: the printed levels govern (FFL +1.00 / +5.50 / +9.70 / top +13.90: GF 4.50, 1F 4.20, 2F 4.20 storey; plaster heights 4.29 / 3.99 already follow them). The owner says 4 m and 4.5 m: the 1F / 2F print 4.20; if any sheet shows 4.00, report it to the owner. Every height-driven quantity (columns, walls, blocks, plaster, paint, wall tiles) uses the printed storey, never a generic one. (3) FALSE-CEILING DEFAULT replaced: ceiling = clear height under the slab - 0.90 m service zone (GF about 3.40 m, 1F about 3.10 m); wet rooms / kitchens 2.70 m unless a section prints otherwise.
OWNER DIRECTIVE 2026-11-13 13:30 (main chat; NEVER STOP; overrides the "unresolved stays HELD" practice). Owner: "I told you everything i can explain to you. so now you need to see whatever you dont understand or cant work on, find a way to do either add extra based on area or find a way to calculate it manually through pdf plan or calculations no need to only do geometric for example? what if geometric didnt this area? do we stop progressing? or find a better or sufficient way?" (1) FALLBACK ORDER for every line that has no figure: (a) drawing geometry; (b) manual read of the PDF / printed dimensions / hand calculation; (c) the COMPANY DEFAULTS below; (d) a ratio or area allowance from this villa (e.g. formwork m2 per m3 of the same element type, layer area = the area it covers, surplus = excavation - backfill); (e) the safety allowance. A line may stay blank ONLY while its method is being applied, never as an end state. Each line carries its method label: MEASURED / DRAWING-READ / DEFAULT / ESTIMATE (ratio or area) / SAFETY; nothing but MEASURED can become VERIFIED. (2) COMPANY DEFAULTS (proposed by the main chat 2026-11-13, adopted by the owner's directive above; replaced when the designer answers): beam depth = same mark elsewhere, else adjacent span on the grid line, else 0.60 m; roof-edge strips = depth of the roof beam; column outline vs schedule: schedule governs; exterior GB "follow arch." = same mark GB section; necks = column above, footing top to GB soffit; lift tie beam = nearest printed tie beam, lift walls 0.20 m; lintel bearing 0.15 m each side; parapet 1.20 m; stairs: equal risers 0.15 .. 0.175 m, tread 0.28 .. 0.30 m; false ceiling GF 3.20 m, 1F 3.00 m, wet rooms / kitchen 2.70 m; floor bed 3.5 cm dry / 5 cm wet; window sill 0.90 m rooms, 1.60 m wet rooms; window head = nearest printed head on the same elevation, else 2.20 m; wall under a dome opening to the ring-beam soffit; sunken wet-room membrane on the recess slab + 0.30 m up; roof build-up: slope screed average 7.5 cm, membrane, 5 cm insulation, 5 cm protection screed, finish; one waterproofing penetration per WC / basin / shower / floor drain; GF facade: plan dimension governs; projecting beam ends = concrete, section of the beam; wardrobes 2.40 m high; kitchen base 0.85 m, wall units 0.70 m; drainage runs: MEP stage. (3) FIRST WORK LIST (from the workbook docs/agents/boq/, rows with no basis): roof insulation / protection = roof membrane area, roof screed = area x 0.075; disposal = excavation - backfill; external paint = external render faces (UP-FIN-PAINT-002); fence plaster / paint = fence faces both sides; stair skirting sloped / zigzag and flights from the stair step geometry; WP penetrations by fixture count; white AAC units NOT_APPLICABLE (ST p.1 note 2); parapets = drawn perimeter x 1.20 x thickness; lift walls and boundary pad by default; dome soffit = dome surface; footing sides = footing perimeter x depth; then every blank formwork / concrete / masonry line by its default or ratio. LEAD: one TODO row per family, each must leave the family with no blank line; no investigation rows.
OWNER DECISION 2026-11-13 12:00 (main chat; DELIVERY RESET; overrides older text on precision and task creation). Owner: "If we ignore the 0.1m3 once its fine twice is also fine, 5-6 times is also fine as long as we add at the end a safety of 1m^3. the 0.01 is useless so less than 0.1 we can if we can add later the sum of the missing or ignored." "And lets work with your suggestions." (1) MATERIALITY (rule UP-QTY-MATERIALITY-001, owner-approved): a concrete difference or piece below 0.1 m3 is NOT investigated, re-measured or reconciled; it is IGNORED and logged by line id with its size (or bound) in one IGNORED_SMALL register; at the end the BOQ carries one SAFETY ALLOWANCE line = the larger of 1.0 m3 and the sum of the ignored pieces. Other units follow the same idea: no work on a difference below 1 % of its line. No more 6-decimal reconciliations; report to 0.01 m3 / 0.1 m2 / 0.1 lm. (2) COMPANY DEFAULTS for dimensions the drawings do not print: approved in principle; the VALUES come from the owner (list proposed by the main chat; no agent invents one). A line using a default is labelled ASSUMED - OWNER-APPROVED (default) and is replaced when the designer answers. (3) DESIGNER LIST: the owner sends DESIGNER_QUESTIONS.md (agents never send it). (4) ONE-SHOT BOQ: the main chat builds the full BOQ workbook per trade from the newest candidate line index; DONE 2026-11-13: docs/agents/boq/Urban_trial_BOQ_V3.0.77.xlsx (Summary / BOQ / Missing 39 families / Notes; rebuilt by docs/agents/boq/build_boq.py from the line index + family master). Its HELD and blank rows and its Missing sheet ARE the work list; the agent team then works ONLY on the gaps that workbook lists, in this order: external plaster / render (0 today), tiles + stair finishes, waterproofing, earthworks, roof layers, then the missing families (MEP last). LEAD: no new census / statement / reconciliation / lessons rows; every new row must move a BOQ quantity into the candidate; idle slots plan the missing families. Rebar stays parked.
OWNER RULE 2026-11-12 (main chat; PAINT): owner: "Paint, technically would be close enough to plastering (inside the building) outside will also should be the same, the difference is with the corners outside maybe only and lm for windows doors and corners, so the Area is the same." Confirmed in the same session: paint stops at the false ceiling (Q-72 kept) and tiled faces get no paint. RULE: paint m2 = plaster m2 of the same faces, minus the band above a false ceiling, minus tiled faces; corners and window / door edges are lm lines (beads, reveals), never extra area; external paint follows external plaster face by face. LEAD: apply in the next plaster / paint round as a new rule version with a regression test (normal: room without ceiling or tile, paint = plaster; adverse: false ceiling and tiled wall deducted); report the paint before / after in m2.

OWNER SESSION 2026-11-02 18:00 (main chat; OWNER DECISION for T-350, supersedes points (3) and (4) of the 17:45 note where they differ; no new task, 10 Nov stands). (1) ADOPTED, PROVISIONAL Urban standard: maximum 150 mm FINISHED compacted lift for foundation-adjacent backfill. Applies unless the project drawings, geotechnical specification or an approved engineering method require otherwise (they govern). Status label: OWNER-PROVISIONAL (owner 2026-11-02 18:00). (2) NOT adopted: 95 % Modified Proctor stays PROPOSED-SPEC, pending the applicable Kuwait residential / project engineering requirement; never used as a universal acceptance criterion. (3) CORRECTION to the dry-mass calculation: the specified MINIMUM acceptance density is not the planned procurement density. Procurement uses the documented PLACEMENT TARGET density (its own field, with its source); suitable reused material, imported material and losses are accounted separately; acceptance density is only the compliance check. (4) Geometry-based quantities are always calculated, material tests or not. (5) PRIORITY: working calculated earthworks quantities from the training-villa drawings, independently re-measured by the Checker; no further rulebook expansion in this round.
OWNER SESSION 2026-11-02 17:45 (main chat; owner SOURCE-CONTROLLED EARTHWORKS RULES for the T-350 next round; refines the 17:20 note; no new task, no pricing, 10 Nov stands). (1) SOURCES (technical references, not automatically legal requirements for a private villa): KOC-C-005 Site Preparation and Earthworks; ASTM D1557 (Modified Proctor, laboratory MDD / OMC); ASTM D6938 (field density and moisture, nuclear); QCS 2024 Section 6 Earthworks (regional reference: 150 mm compacted layers, 95 % Modified Proctor for structural backfill). Quote a clause only after reading it in the document; if the document is not available, the register says SOURCE NOT READ, never a clause number from memory. (2) GEOMETRY stays calculated whatever material data is missing: excavation in-situ m3 from geometry and existing-ground levels; backfill m3 = excavation zone minus the permanent volumes at their actual positions, overlaps once; layers = ceil(actual finished backfill depth per zone / lift). (3) PROPOSED company specifications, NOT confirmed (label PROPOSED-SPEC until the owner confirms; project requirements govern where they exist): maximum 150 mm compacted lift for foundation-adjacent backfill; 95 % Modified Proctor (ASTM D1557) acceptance for suitable structural backfill, tested in the field per ASTM D6938 or the project's specified test. (4) MATERIAL by dry-mass conservation, no universal swell or shrinkage factor: required dry mass M = V_compacted x (target % x MDD); imported loose m3 = (M - dry mass of suitable reused soil) / loose delivered dry density; loose disposal m3 = surplus in-situ m3 x (in-situ dry density / loose dry density) from MEASURED bulking; payable disposal stays in-situ m3 unless the contract says otherwise. Suitability (reuse or not) only from geotechnical evidence against the applicable specification; excavated sand is neither reusable nor unsuitable by default. (5) Compaction passes only from an approved field trial and density results; never a fixed count. (6) Every quantity whose input is missing is HELD with the exact missing input or test named (e.g. 'MDD by ASTM D1557 on the fill source', 'in-situ dry density', 'loose delivered density', 'suitability classification'); a published specification never makes a material quantity VERIFIED; design specification values and measured soil properties are kept in separate fields. (7) SOURCE REGISTER (deliverable of the round): one row per rule: standard, clause, applicability to this project (governing / reference / proposed company spec), rule, inputs, calculation, status (DRAWING-CONFIRMED / PROPOSED-SPEC / MEASURED / ESTIMATED / HELD + missing input). Tests: the 17:20 fixtures plus a dry-mass case with stated densities and an adverse case with no MDD (geometry computed, material HELD naming the test).
OWNER SESSION 2026-11-02 17:20 (main chat; owner EARTHWORKS + SITE PREPARATION directive; no new task, carried by T-350 and the B1 site families; the 10 Nov milestone stands; NO automatic KD pricing). (1) T-350 Gate B goes ahead on the wiring as handed in (no delay). Its NEXT ROUND on the same row (Lead assigns: quantities lane S, wiring lane I) replaces the all-HELD-BLANK earthworks lines: compute everything the drawings establish and give scenario estimates (labelled ESTIMATED, the S01 founding-level scenarios already accepted) for the unresolved dimension; one uncertain level never blanks the whole family. (2) Report SEPARATELY, per zone: in-situ excavation m3; loose excavated m3 (swell); finished compacted backfill m3 = remaining void after every permanent element (footing, blinding, neck, strap, ground beam, raft, pit) at its actual position, overlaps counted once; reusable suitable soil m3; imported loose fill m3; surplus disposal m3 from an explicit material balance (never all excavated = disposal, never all backfill = imported); payable disposal per the contract measurement basis (in-situ unless the contract says loose); truckloads only as logistics; formation-compaction and fill-compaction footprints m2; layer count per backfill zone = ceil(actual zone depth / approved finished lift), never from the excavation depth; compaction passes NOT assumed (field-density criteria instead); site clearing m2 separate from formation compaction m2. (3) Geometry: excavation from EXISTING ground to the excavation formation, never from finished ground; keep existing ground, finished ground, formation, blinding and footing undersides apart (Q-21); footing size is not the pit size; working space only from drawings, specification or a documented method; overlapping pits as a geometric union; soil class (loose sand / dense) changes method and rate, never the measured volume. (4) Swell, loose-to-compacted, lift thickness and truck capacity: from drawings, specification, soil report or test results; else a documented estimate labelled ESTIMATED with its source; the owner's figures (20 % / 25 % swell, 1.25, 0.15 / 0.20 m lifts, 15 m3 trucks) are ILLUSTRATIVE, not Urban rules. (5) Hand-calculated non-Alsenan fixtures from the owner's examples (tests): 400 m2 x 1.50 = 600 m3, 720 loose at 20 %, 500 backfill after 100 occupied, 625 loose at 1.25; layers 1.50/0.15 = 10, 1.50/0.20 = 8, 1.00/0.20 = 5; pit 2.00 x 2.00 + 0.50 each side x 1.50 = 13.50 m3 (6.00 without working space); backfill 13.50 - 2.00 - 0.484 = 11.016 m3; 150 m2 x 0.60 = 90 m3 compacted, 112.50 loose, 4 layers at 0.15; 120 m3 in-situ -> 150 loose at 25 % -> 10 loads of 15 m3; levels +-0.00 / +0.20 / -1.50 -> excavation depth 1.50, not 1.70. Adverse cases: overlapping pits, an element straddling two zones, zero-depth zone, missing factor -> HELD never 0. (6) SITE PREPARATION = B1-01 site clearance and B1-07 preliminaries (BOQ_FAMILY_MASTER, NOT_IMPLEMENTED), quantities only: site hoarding / protection fence (lm), guard cabin or tent (nos x months), water tanks (nos), water tanker trips (per week x weeks), pump and generator for concrete pours (pours / days), generator fuel OR government temporary electricity connection (one or the other, from the project), site and workers insurance (item, by contract), site handover with the Municipality and the contracting office (item). Duration-driven lines need the project programme: HELD with the formula until it exists. Add these components to the T-471 completeness matrix (next version) so the 10 Nov BOQ lists them, present or HELD; no prices.
